## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- ai4rag is a Kubernetes component.
- connecting to external services\: milvus, openai, s3.
- runtime dependencies\: S3, my-svc.
- 20 Python package dependencies (20 required), categories\: 1 cloud-provider, 1 data, 1 llm-sdk, 1 serialization.
- Python k8s client with 16 API call(s).
